HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001175/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0696746868fb17c123a2","text":"2. **Productive cleaving distinguished from destructive cutting.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ف ل ح/B001` is opening by cleaving; 91:8 `ف ج ر/B001` is splitting into outflow, while 91:14 `ع ق ر/B002` cuts an animal down. **Structural cue and role:** the success verb stands between a potentially life-opening split and a later life-disabling cut, assigning `أفلح` the role of opening constriction without severing vitality. **Abductive move:** the text does not explicitly classify cuts; the distinction between productive and annihilative openings is inferred from their outcomes. **Reading change:** success entails knowing how to open a field for life rather than treating every barrier-breaking act as liberation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0a7af89c94906b75734a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:9:4\",\"root_000637\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Arındırma, önceden dengeli kurulmuş canın iç düzgünlüğünü koruyan ve onun bildiği ayrımları yeniden görünür kılan bir onarımdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B005","root_000766/B001","root_000766/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2b6e17bb38847dfa8c57","text":"3. **Pair/evenness branch makes success relationally balanced.** Lexical evidence: ز ك و B005 \"pair and even number\"; س و ي B001 equality/equivalence and B006 even meeting place; ف ج ر/و ق ي paired in 91:8. Structural cue: فجورها وتقواها are a pair, and the preceding cosmic oaths also come in paired contrasts. Assigned role: زكو's pair/even branch turns purification into proper handling of dual capacities. Abductive move: I infer that the successful person does not erase the pair but orders it; the packet supplies evenness, pairing, and moral opposites. Reading change: زكّىها can be read as bringing the self's two exposed possibilities into right proportion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3612baff60b730c003ed","text":"The later narrative made `زكّىها` social and ecological, not only inward. Respecting the she-camel's water-share in 91:13 is a public form of guarding growth; عقر in 91:14 is the anti-zakā act, cutting growth/source and producing barrenness. This retrospectively made فلاح's agricultural branch central rather than decorative.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_001175/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_409922a1df7759d71d73","text":"1. **Success as sustained cultivation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ف ل ح/B003` is the tiller who splits earth and fixed `ز ك و/B001` is growth and increase; 91:6 supplies fertile spread ground (`ء ر ض/B002`), 91:13 a water share (`س ق ي/B003`), and 91:14 cutting the source of growth (`ع ق ر/B007`). **Structural cue and role:** ground, tending, growth, watering, and later destructive cutting form one material mechanism; `زكاها` is the cultivated yield. **Abductive move:** the elements are supplied, while the causal chain from preparation and watering to growth is inferred. **Reading change:** flourishing is a maintained ecological practice, not a one-time declaration of success.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001175/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_548051a14bd4278ca689","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:9:2\",\"root_001175\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Başarının toprağı yararak açan yönü, yaşamı çoğaltan üretken bir işlemi ileride devenin yaşam kaynağını kesen yıkıcı müdahaleden ayırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B002","root_000766/B002","root_001677/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6812045afd2c05c1489f","text":"2. **Purification as restoring internal straightness.** Lexical evidence: ز ك و B002 \"purity and rectitude\"; س و ي B002 \"internal straightness and sound completion\" in 91:7; و ق ي B002 protective caution in 91:8. Structural cue: the soul was made even before it was morally inspired; now someone performs زكّىها. Assigned role: purification protects and restores the soul's original sound constitution. Abductive move: I infer that moral action is maintenance of a given design rather than self-invention; the packet supplies proportioning, guarding, and purification branches. Reading change: the ayah reads as keeping faith with an already well-made self.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001035/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7419fc81790969e3a8cb","text":"91:13-14 supplied the missing material completion of the cultivation reading: a water share sustains the animal order, while hamstringing activates `ع ق ر/B007`, cutting off regrowth. This made flourishing depend not only on inward cleansing but on preserving the conditions from which life continues.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7e9c2e0ad4c40b6c45f5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:9:4\",\"root_000637\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Çift ve denklik boyutu, arındırmayı canın iki ahlaki imkânını silmek değil, onları doğru ölçüye yerleştirmek olarak düşündürebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_000637/B001","root_001175/B003","root_001175/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a763b8f4b28468b055a5","text":"1. **Success as cultivation of the fashioned self.** Lexical evidence: ف ل ح B003 \"tiller who splits the ground\"; ف ل ح B005 \"success with lasting good\"; ز ك و B001 \"growth and increase\"; ء ر ض B002 fertile ground in 91:6. Structural cue: after earth-spreading and soul-fashioning, the one who purifies/increases it succeeds. Assigned role: falah joins agricultural cutting to lasting attainment; zaka makes the self grow well. Abductive move: I infer that success comes from properly working the soul as cultivated ground; the packet supplies tilling, growth, and fertile earth branches. Reading change: the ayah reads as disciplined cultivation, not a purely forensic declaration of success.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B001","root_001175/B003","root_001175/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d53e4f043f69f1b2f83e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:9:2\",\"root_001175\",[\"B003\",\"B005\"]],[\"91:9:4\",\"root_000637\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Başarı, canı verimli toprak gibi sürekli emekle işleyip büyütmek ve onun yaşamı sürdüren koşullarını korumaktır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ec13f8d1e0de86316f2f","text":"3. **Purification as bringing the self out of cover.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ز ك و/B002` is purification and rectitude; 91:4 `غ ش و/B001` covers, 91:10 `د س و/B001` conceals, and 91:11 and 91:14 use `ك ذ ب/B002` to deny or declare false. **Structural cue and role:** `زكاها` is the positive center of a visibility sequence whose negative side hides the self and then denies an external warning. **Abductive move:** the packet gives cover, purification, concealment, and denial; treating purification as restored visibility is inferred. **Reading change:** self-purification becomes an anti-concealment practice that lets acknowledged distinctions remain visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
